## LLM Infrastructure

### [v0.24.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.24.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>vLLM v0.24.0 Release Notes</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 571 commits from 256 contributors (77 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>MiniMax-M3</strong>: Added support for the new <strong>MiniMax-M3</strong> model (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45381">#45381</a>), with a fast follow-on of BF16/FP8 indexer via MSA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45892">#45892</a>), MXFP4 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45896">#45896</a>), FP8 sparse GQA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45744">#45744</a>), and extensive AMD/ROCm tuning — mxfp8 MoE/linear on gfx950 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45725">#45725</a>), fp8_per_channel for bf16 weights on MI300X (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45854">#45854</a>), FP8 KV-cache fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45720">#45720</a>), and packed-modules mapping (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45794">#45794</a>). A MiniMax-M2 perf regression was also fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45935">#45935</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4 keeps maturing</strong>: Following its debut, DeepSeek-V4 received another large optimization pass — a FlashInfer sparse index cache (2–4% TTFT) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45863">#45863</a>), prefill chunk-planning optimization (4% E2E throughput) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45061">#45061</a>), a cluster-cooperative topK kernel for low-latency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43008">#43008</a>), contiguous per-block KV allocations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44577">#44577</a>), TEP=16 for the block-FP8 shared expert (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46001">#46001</a>), and native DSA indexer decode for <code>next_n &gt; 2</code> on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45322">#45322</a>). It is now enabled on <strong>SM120</strong> alongside GLM-5.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43477">#43477</a>), with XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44144">#44144</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44517">#44517</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45240">#45240</a>) and ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44899">#44899</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45103">#45103</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45681">#45681</a>) attention/MoE paths added.</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 (MRv2) continues to expand</strong>: MRv2 now <strong>supports quantized models by default</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44446">#44446</a>), enables <strong>GraniteMoE by default</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45461">#45461</a>), and gained migration of Qwen + DeepSeek-V2 MoE models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42667">#42667</a>), DFlash speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44586">#44586</a>), and more accurate FP32 Gumbel sampling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45996">#45996</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Streaming Parser Engine</strong>: A new streaming parser engine unifies tool-call/reasoning parsing across models, with parsers for Qwen3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45413">#45413</a>), MiniMax-M2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45701">#45701</a>), GLM-4.7/5.1/5.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45915">#45915</a>), and Nemotron V3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45755">#45755</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Diffusion LLMs</strong>: Added <strong>DiffusionGemma</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45163">#45163</a>), including a CPU path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45690">#45690</a>) and structured-output guardrails for diffusion decoders (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45468">#45468</a>).</li>
<li><strong>WideEP / DeepEP v2</strong>: Integrated <strong>DeepEP v2</strong> for expert parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41183">#41183</a>), with follow-on robustness fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46404">#46404</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46432">#46432</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend matures further</strong>: Added API-key authentication (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44321">#44321</a>), CORS (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45753">#45753</a>), <code>/tokenize</code> + <code>/detokenize</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44222">#44222</a>), <code>/pause</code> <code>/resume</code> <code>/is_paused</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44499">#44499</a>), <code>/abort_requests</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44382">#44382</a>), <code>/get_world_size</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44801">#44801</a>), <code>thinking_token_budget</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46137">#46137</a>), a Python bridge for Rust tool parsers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44624">#44624</a>), and many new parsers and validation paths.</li>
<li><strong>Device selection change</strong>: vLLM no longer sets <code>CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES</code> internally; a new <code>device_ids</code> argument is provided instead (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45026">#45026</a>). On ROCm, a deprecation window for <code>CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES</code> has begun (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46636">#46636</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Support</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>New models</strong>: MiniMax-M3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45381">#45381</a>), DiffusionGemma (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45163">#45163</a>) + Gemma Diffusion on CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45690">#45690</a>), Hierarchical Reasoning Model — Text / HrmTextForCausalLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43098">#43098</a>), OpenMOSS (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44124">#44124</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Gemma 4</strong>: Unified FlashAttention (FA4) across all layers + <code>mm_prefix</code> support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42175">#42175</a>); many parser/serving fixes — forced-JSON skip for required/named tool choice (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45795">#45795</a>), parsing with thinking disabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45832">#45832</a>), streaming reasoning-state init (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45852">#45852</a>), reasoning rendering on assistant turns (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45867">#45867</a>), offline-parser truncation/token-leak fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45553">#45553</a>); legacy Gemma4 parsers replaced with an engine-based implementation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45588">#45588</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4</strong>: OOM fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44914">#44914</a>), MTP projection prefixing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44821">#44821</a>), supported KV-cache dtypes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44892">#44892</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Qwen / multimodal</strong>: Qwen3-VL video loader (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44412">#44412</a>), Qwen2-VL/Qwen2.5-VL processor-mapped video loader (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45555">#45555</a>), Qwen3-VL multi-video processing optimization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46026">#46026</a>) and multi-video crash fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46305">#46305</a>), Qwen3-Omni VIT cu_seqlens device fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44264">#44264</a>), fused qk-rmsnorm-rope-gate for Qwen3.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44176">#44176</a>), Qwen3.5 EP weight-loading fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45002">#45002</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ViT full CUDA graph</strong>: GLM-4.1V (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40576">#40576</a>), DeepSeek-OCR dual-path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43586">#43586</a>), Kimi-VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41992">#41992</a>), mllama4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40660">#40660</a>), Lfm2VL encoder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44930">#44930</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Other model fixes</strong>: Llama4 weight loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45047">#45047</a>) and streamed loading to avoid host-OOM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44645">#44645</a>), MiMo v2.x QKV TP sharding + FP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45200">#45200</a>), ColQwen3.5 retrieval correctness (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46108">#46108</a>), EXAONE-4.5 vision encoder (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45073">#45073</a>), MiDashengLM TP&gt;1 audio-encoder crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44408">#44408</a>), MiniCPM-o/V device-placement and image-size fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43844">#43844</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42332">#42332</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44980">#44980</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45244">#45244</a>), Cohere2 MoE weight loading + parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44747">#44747</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44907">#44907</a>), Nemotron V3 reasoning-as-content (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39091">#39091</a>), ColBERT AutoWeightsLoader + query/document embedding io processor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44999">#44999</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45210">#45210</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kernels</strong>: GLM-5 TRT-LLM ragged MLA prefill dimensions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43525">#43525</a>), GLM-5 router GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46385">#46385</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Engine Core</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: Quantized models by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44446">#44446</a>), GraniteMoE default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45461">#45461</a>), Qwen/DSv2 MoE migration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42667">#42667</a>), DFlash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44586">#44586</a>), simplified async output handling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45442">#45442</a>), attention-group split on <code>num_heads_q</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45564">#45564</a>), LoRA warmup fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35536">#35536</a>), more accurate FP32 Gumbel sampling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45996">#45996</a>), <code>min_tokens</code> off-by-one fix in the V2 GPU sampler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46243">#46243</a>), plus assorted model/config compatibility fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45868">#45868</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding</strong>: Dynamic SD (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/32374">#32374</a>); DFlash with FlashInfer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43081">#43081</a>), mixed KV page sizes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45181">#45181</a>), and Qwen3Next targets (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45319">#45319</a>); EAGLE3 support for Qwen3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43132">#43132</a>); reduced TP communication for large-vocab drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39419">#39419</a>); race fix in async accepted counts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45100">#45100</a>); EAGLE multimodal encoder cache fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46315">#46315</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV cache &amp; scheduler</strong>: KV-cache watermark to reduce preemptions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44594">#44594</a>), two-phase allocation for cross-group prefix-cache hits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44409">#44409</a>), Marconi-style admission policy for hybrid cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/37898">#37898</a>), prefix-cache retention for Mamba/linear attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45845">#45845</a>), DS Mamba tail-copy for MTP align mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45473">#45473</a>), reduced scheduler copy overhead (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45840">#45840</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Attention</strong>: Re-enabled cross-layer KV cache layout for MLA via stride-aware kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45111">#45111</a>), MLA prefill FA4 fp8 output (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43050">#43050</a>), FlexAttention custom mask mods made fully cudagraphable (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45232">#45232</a>), triton diff-kv backend for MiMo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41797">#41797</a>), FlashMLA sparse accuracy fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36616">#36616</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Weight loading &amp; core</strong>: fastsafetensors <code>ParallelLoader</code> for weight loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40183">#40183</a>), release of cached device memory under pressure on UMA GPUs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45179">#45179</a>), structured outputs for beam search (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35022">#35022</a>), <code>device_ids</code> arg / no internal <code>CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45026">#45026</a>), graceful fallback when <code>numactl --membind</code> is blocked (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45438">#45438</a>), config-class registration before tokenizer init (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40299">#40299</a>), async scheduling with prompt embeds for multimodal models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45673">#45673</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Large Scale Serving &amp; Distributed</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Expert parallel</strong>: DeepEP v2 integration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41183">#41183</a>) with token-bound and topk-index fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46404">#46404</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46432">#46432</a>); NIXL EP — DBO with NIXL EP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45275">#45275</a>), top-k index dtype query (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45298">#45298</a>), NVFP4 post-receive quantization skip (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45606">#45606</a>), elastic-EP communicator (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45013">#45013</a>); reject NCCL-based EPLB with async EPLB (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44978">#44978</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV connectors / disaggregated serving</strong>: KV push from prefill to decode via NIXL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35264">#35264</a>); per-region KV transfer classification for mixed full-attn + MLA groups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44583">#44583</a>); Mooncake pipeline-parallel PD support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44528">#44528</a>), async lookup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45659">#45659</a>), compact chunk-hash zero-copy lookup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45969">#45969</a>), SWA-block skipping (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45444">#45444</a>); P/D fixes with DP supervisor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46628">#46628</a>) and DSV4 disaggregation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45831">#45831</a>); removed <code>P2pNcclConnector</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44854">#44854</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV offloading</strong>: Multi-tier async batched lookup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44193">#44193</a>), packed HMA KV-cache layout (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46205">#46205</a>, gated <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46252">#46252</a>), parallel-agnostic fs-tier cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44733">#44733</a>), offloading-manager stats (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35669">#35669</a>) and labeled/CPU-usage metrics (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45957">#45957</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45737">#45737</a>), self-describing KV events (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43468">#43468</a>), non-blocking idle flush (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45595">#45595</a>), and numerous correctness/race fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44784">#44784</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45823">#45823</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46231">#46231</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46278">#46278</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Distributed core</strong>: Prefill step cadence for better non-PD DP balancing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44558">#44558</a>), KV-event map encoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42892">#42892</a>), one-shot fused all-reduce PDL NaN fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45448">#45448</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Hardware &amp; Performance</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA / kernels</strong>: SM90 CUTLASS FP8 mm odd-M support via swap_ab (180–290% kernel speedup) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44572">#44572</a>), tuned <code>fused_moe</code> FP8 for Qwen3-Next-80B on H100 (+25%) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44830">#44830</a>), native DSA indexer decode on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45322">#45322</a>), cluster-cooperative topK for DeepSeek low-latency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43008">#43008</a>), PDL support for DeepGEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46006">#46006</a>), FlashInfer cutedsl NVFP4 GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42235">#42235</a>) and cute-dsl MXFP8 linear kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46393">#46393</a>), new Helion kernels for FP8/RMSNorm quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36902">#36902</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/33790">#33790</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/36895">#36895</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/34432">#34432</a>).</li>
<li><strong>torch stable ABI</strong>: Continued (and completed) migration of kernels to the libtorch stable ABI — MoE [10c/n] (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44565">#44565</a>), Marlin [11a/n] (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45176">#45176</a>), Machete [11b/n] (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45304">#45304</a>), final <code>_C</code> library migration [12/n] (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45415">#45415</a>).</li>
<li><strong>AMD ROCm</strong>: Torch 2.11 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45362">#45362</a>); fused AR + RMSNorm + per-group FP8 quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42864">#42864</a>), fused softplus-sqrt-topk MoE router under AITER (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44945">#44945</a>), DSv4 flash-decode split-K kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44899">#44899</a>) and inverse-RoPE fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45103">#45103</a>), W4A16 FlyDSL MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44400">#44400</a>), A8W4 MoE CDNA4 swizzle gate for gpt-oss (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44804">#44804</a>); deprecation window begun for <code>CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES</code> on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46636">#46636</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Intel XPU</strong>: Sequence-parallel support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/38608">#38608</a>), torch-xpu 2.12 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42262">#42262</a>), vllm-xpu-kernels v0.1.10 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40367">#40367</a>), W4A16 int4 group_size=32 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45136">#45136</a>), DeepSeek-V4 attention/MoE paths (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44144">#44144</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44517">#44517</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45240">#45240</a>), top-p sampling correctness fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44470">#44470</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CPU &amp; other architectures</strong>: 2.5× faster ASR CPU preprocessing via multi-threading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44612">#44612</a>), CPU W4A16 INT4 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43409">#43409</a>), cgroup memory-limit-aware KV cache sizing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45086">#45086</a>), RISC-V oneDNN W8A8 INT8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44478">#44478</a>) and RVV micro-GEMM for WNA16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44324">#44324</a>), pinned memory for WSL2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41496">#41496</a>), ZenCPU runtime logging (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42726">#42726</a>).</li>
<li><strong>TPU</strong>: tpu-inference upgraded to v0.22.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45793">#45793</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Misc perf</strong>: <code>VLLM_TRITON_FORCE_FIRST_CONFIG</code> to skip Triton autotuning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42425">#42425</a>), Triton recompile detection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45631">#45631</a>), fused multi-group block-table staged writes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44944">#44944</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Quantization</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Online &amp; mixed-precision</strong>: Online FP8 per-token-per-channel (PTPC) quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44132">#44132</a>); <code>modelopt_mixed</code> support extended to Ampere/SM80-86 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45306">#45306</a>) and Turing/SM75 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45375">#45375</a>).</li>
<li><strong>FP4 / MXFP</strong>: FlashInfer cutedsl NVFP4 GEMM backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42235">#42235</a>) and cute-dsl MXFP8 linear kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46393">#46393</a>), MXFP4 W4A4 MoE CUTLASS E8M0 scale fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43557">#43557</a>), SwiGLU clamp wired for NVFP4 MoE on non-Blackwell (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45836">#45836</a>), <code>flashinfer_cutlass</code> allowed as a clamped NVFP4 MoE backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46492">#46492</a>), NVFP4/OCP MX MoE emulation fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46254">#46254</a>), FP8 MoE re-enabled on NVIDIA Thor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46339">#46339</a>).</li>
<li><strong>GGUF / compressed-tensors / AWQ</strong>: GGUF quantization migrated to a plugin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39612">#39612</a>), compressed-tensors WNA16 MoE actorder fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41161">#41161</a>) and KV-cache-scheme rejection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45312">#45312</a>), AWQ format on XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43404">#43404</a>) and AWQ dequantize fix on Intel XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42727">#42727</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kernels &amp; correctness</strong>: QuantizedActivation linear-kernel contract (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44260">#44260</a>), consolidated Marlin thread-tile padding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45295">#45295</a>), FP8 weight layout canonicalized to (K, N) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44735">#44735</a>), corrupt-output fix for MoE FP8 with LoRAs loaded (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42120">#42120</a>), symmetric-quant regression fix in GPTQ/CT MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45656">#45656</a>), <code>fp8_e5m2</code> KV cache allowed for non-fp8 checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45040">#45040</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>API &amp; Frontend</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Tool calling &amp; parsing</strong>: Strict mode for tool calling in Chat Completions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45003">#45003</a>) and Responses API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45396">#45396</a>); new Streaming Parser Engine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45413">#45413</a>) with Qwen3, MiniMax-M2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45701">#45701</a>), GLM-4.7/5.1/5.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45915">#45915</a>), Nemotron V3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45755">#45755</a>) parsers; unified Parser consolidation in chat serving (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45548">#45548</a>); numerous parser correctness fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46047">#46047</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46091">#46091</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46159">#46159</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45763">#45763</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46351">#46351</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43984">#43984</a>).</li>
<li><strong>OpenAI / Responses</strong>: Real <code>/v1/embeddings</code> support for messages + <code>chat_template_kwargs</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45173">#45173</a>), multimodal token counts in <code>usage.prompt_tokens_details</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45458">#45458</a>), omit empty <code>tool_calls</code> from chat responses (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44105">#44105</a>), Responses API streaming <code>function_call</code> id fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44608">#44608</a>), Harmony refactor of streaming/non-streaming paths (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45171">#45171</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45104">#45104</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Anthropic Messages API</strong>: Cache-usage reporting in <code>/v1/messages</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40912">#40912</a>), mid-conversation system-message handling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46025">#46025</a>), inline system-message position preserved for prefix caching (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44602">#44602</a>), <code>tool_use</code> argument-dropping fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45287">#45287</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend</strong>: API-key auth (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44321">#44321</a>), CORS (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45753">#45753</a>), <code>/tokenize</code> + <code>/detokenize</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44222">#44222</a>), <code>/pause</code> <code>/resume</code> <code>/is_paused</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44499">#44499</a>), <code>/abort_requests</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44382">#44382</a>), <code>/get_world_size</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44801">#44801</a>), <code>thinking_token_budget</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46137">#46137</a>), <code>parallel_tool_calls=false</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44760">#44760</a>), continuous usage stats (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43965">#43965</a>), model metadata in <code>/v1/models</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45950">#45950</a>), Python bridge for Rust tool parsers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44624">#44624</a>), dedicated runtime for HTTP/ZMQ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46051">#46051</a>), and many validation/correctness fixes.</li>
<li><strong>Metrics</strong>: <code>vllm:tool_call_parser_invocations_total</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44448">#44448</a>), group-aware KV cache capacity in <code>vllm:cache_config_info</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42206">#42206</a>), MLA attention metrics for DeepSeek MFU estimation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/39457">#39457</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Pooling / embeddings</strong>: Validation for Cohere <code>/v2/embed</code> input exclusivity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45640">#45640</a>), non-negative rerank <code>top_n</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46119">#46119</a>), matryoshka embedding dimension bounds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46313">#46313</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Benchmarks</strong>: BFCL tool-calling dataset for <code>vllm bench serve</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42457">#42457</a>), multi-turn benchmark api_key/custom headers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44516">#44516</a>), tokenizer-mismatch auto-correction (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44708">#44708</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Security</h3>
<p>This release ships another coordinated security-hardening batch (much of it from security researcher <a class="user-mention notranslate" href="https://github.com/jperezdealgaba">@jperezdealgaba</a>).</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Denial of service</strong>: Audio decompression bomb in the speech-to-text endpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44970">#44970</a>), remote DoS via invalid recovered-token reinjection in speculative decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44744">#44744</a>), DoS via <code>prompt_embeds</code> on M-RoPE models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45252">#45252</a>), regex-compilation timeout guard in structured outputs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45118">#45118</a>), audio upload size limit before full materialization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45510">#45510</a>), audio decode duration limit in the chat-completions path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45908">#45908</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Information disclosure</strong>: int32 truncation in the GGUF dequantize kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44971">#44971</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Input validation &amp; hardening</strong>: Image EXIF orientation and tRNS transparency handling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44974">#44974</a>), rejection of non-finite <code>temperature</code>/<code>repetition_penalty</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45116">#45116</a>), <code>sanitize_message</code> applied to Anthropic and STT error paths (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45119">#45119</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Dependencies</strong>: Upgrade Starlette to ≥ 1.0.1 to fix <a href="https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-86qp-5c8j-p5mr" title="CVE-2026-48710">CVE-2026-48710</a> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45675">#45675</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Dependencies</h3>
<ul>
<li>Torch 2.11 on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45362">#45362</a>), torch-xpu 2.12 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42262">#42262</a>), tpu-inference v0.22.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45793">#45793</a>), NIXL v0.10.1 for XPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40287">#40287</a>), Starlette ≥ 1.0.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45675">#45675</a>).</li>
<li><code>mistral_common</code> is now optional via deferred import (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45305">#45305</a>); CUDA Dockerfiles upgraded from GCC 10 to GCC 12 for C++20 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44923">#44923</a>); spinloop extension skipped on Python &lt; 3.11 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44783">#44783</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Deprecations &amp; Removals</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Removed models</strong>: ERNIE (obsolete) (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45127">#45127</a>), Xverse (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45638">#45638</a>), Dots1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45637">#45637</a>), Bamba (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45990">#45990</a>), Mono-InternVL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45129">#45129</a>), InternLM registry alias (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45128">#45128</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Deprecated</strong>: First-generation Qwen and QwenVL models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45131">#45131</a>), Transformers v4 support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45161">#45161</a>), <code>CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES</code> on ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46636">#46636</a>); general deprecations for v0.23/v0.24 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/44992">#44992</a>).</li>
</ul>
